Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the geometry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 769. The moduli space has dimension at most 828 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 769 + O(1/n^828). Summing over all modes and applying the geometry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
